Find all xmlns declarations\n    ns_pattern = r'xmlns:([^=]+)=\"([^\"]+)\"'\n    matches = re.findall(ns_pattern, content)\n\n    for prefix, uri in matches:\n        ET.register_namespace(prefix, uri)\n\n    # Also check for default namespace (xmlns without prefix)\n    default_ns_pattern = r'xmlns=\"([^\"]+)\"'\n    default_matches = re.findall(default_ns_pattern, content)\n    for uri in default_matches:\n        ET.register_namespace('', uri)\n\n\ndef get_namespace_prefixes(xml_file):\n    \"\"\"Get a dictionary of namespace prefixes from the XML file.\"\"\"\n    with open(xml_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        content = f.read()\n\n    ns_map = {}\n    ns_pattern = r'xmlns:([^=]+)=\"([^\"]+)\"'\n    matches = re.findall(ns_pattern, content)\n    for prefix, uri in matches:\n        ns_map[prefix] = uri\n\n    # Default namespace\n    default_ns_pattern = r'xmlns=\"([^\"]+)\"'\n    default_matches = re.findall(default_ns_pattern, content)\n    for uri in default_matches:\n        ns_map[''] = uri\n\n    return ns_map\n\n\ndef find_text_elements(element, ns_map):\n    \"\"\"Find all text-bearing elements (<hp:t> or similar).\"\"\"\n    text_elements = []\n    hp_ns = ns_map.get('hp', 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML')\n\n    for elem in element.iter():\n        if elem.tag == f'{{{hp_ns}}}t' or elem.tag.endswith('}t'):\n            text_elements.append(elem)\n\n    return text_elements\n\n\ndef find_parent_paragraph(element, ns_map):\n    \"\"\"Find the parent paragraph (<hp:p>) of an element.\"\"\"\n    hp_ns = ns_map.get('hp', 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML')\n    parent = element\n\n    while parent is not None:\n        if parent.tag == f'{{{hp_ns}}}p' or parent.tag.endswith('}p'):\n            return parent\n        parent = parent.find('..') if hasattr(parent, 'find') else None\n\n    return None\n\n\ndef remove_layout_cache(paragraph, ns_map):\n    \"\"\"Remove layout cache elements from a paragraph.\"\"\"\n    hp_ns = ns_map.get('hp', 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML')\n\n    # Find and remove linesegarray, lineseg elements\n    to_remove = []\n    for child in list(paragraph):\n        tag_local = child.tag.split('}')[-1] if '}' in child.tag else child.tag\n        if 'lineseg' in tag_local.lower() or 'linesegarray' in tag_local.lower():\n            to_remove.append(child)\n\n    for child in to_remove:\n        paragraph.remove(child)\n\n\ndef process_section(xml_file, update_data, followups, modified_paragraphs):\n    \"\"\"Process a section XML file and apply updates.\"\"\"\n    # Register namespaces first\n    register_namespaces(xml_file)\n    ns_map = get_namespace_prefixes(xml_file)\n    hp_ns = ns_map.get('hp', 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML')\n\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_file)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    # Build old value mappings\n    old_customer = \"Northwind Retail\"\n    old_owner = \"이전담당자\"\n    old_window = \"2025-04-01 ~ 2025-04-15\"\n    new_window = f\"{update_data['window_start']} ~ {update_data['window_end']}\"\n    old_band = \"Standard-18M\"\n    old_escalation = \"ops-old@northwind.example\"\n    old_note_prefix = \"가격 메모: \"\n    old_note_content = \"기존 메모를 새 메모로 바꾸세요.\"\n\n    # Follow-up mappings (old values)\n    old_followups = [\n        \"1. old follow-up alpha\",\n        \"2. old follow-up beta\",\n        \"3. old follow-up gamma\"\n    ]\n\n    # Build parent mapping for all elements\n    parent_map = {}\n    for parent in root.iter():\n        for child in parent:\n            parent_map[child] = parent\n\n    # Track which paragraphs were modified\n    modified_p_ids = set()\n\n    # Find all text elements and their parent paragraphs\n    for para in root.iter(f'{{{hp_ns}}}p'):\n        para_id = para.get('id')\n        text_content = \"\"\n        text_elems_in_para = []\n\n        # Collect all text elements in this paragraph\n        for run in para.iter(f'{{{hp_ns}}}run'):\n            for t_elem in run.iter(f'{{{hp_ns}}}t'):\n                if t_elem.text:\n                    text_content += t_elem.text\n                text_elems_in_para.append(t_elem)\n\n        if not text_elems_in_para:\n            continue\n\n        modified = False\n\n        # Check if this paragraph contains text we need to update\n        full_text = \"\".join(te.text or \"\" for te in text_elems_in_para)\n\n        # Skip the appendix preservation sentence\n        if \"이 부록 문단은 그대로 유지해야 합니다.\" in full_text:\n            continue\n\n        # Replace customer name (Northwind Retail)\n        if old_customer in full_text:\n            # Clear and rebuild text elements\n            new_text = full_text.replace(old_customer, update_data['customer'])\n            if len(text_elems_in_para) == 1:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n            else:\n                # Multiple runs - combine into first, clear others\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                for te in text_elems_in_para[1:]:\n                    te.text = \"\"\n            modified = True\n\n        # Replace owner\n        elif old_owner in full_text:\n            new_text = full_text.replace(old_owner, update_data['owner_name'])\n            if len(text_elems_in_para) == 1:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n            else:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                for te in text_elems_in_para[1:]:\n                    te.text = \"\"\n            modified = True\n\n        # Replace window dates\n        elif old_window in full_text:\n            new_text = full_text.replace(old_window, new_window)\n            if len(text_elems_in_para) == 1:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n            else:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                for te in text_elems_in_para[1:]:\n                    te.text = \"\"\n            modified = True\n\n        # Replace pricing band\n        elif old_band in full_text:\n            new_text = full_text.replace(old_band, update_data['band'])\n            if len(text_elems_in_para) == 1:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n            else:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                for te in text_elems_in_para[1:]:\n                    te.text = \"\"\n            modified = True\n\n        # Replace escalation email\n        elif old_escalation in full_text:\n            new_text = full_text.replace(old_escalation, update_data['escalation_email'])\n            if len(text_elems_in_para) == 1:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n            else:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                for te in text_elems_in_para[1:]:\n                    te.text = \"\"\n            modified = True\n\n        # Replace pricing note\n        elif old_note_content in full_text:\n            new_text = full_text.replace(old_note_content, update_data['note'])\n            if len(text_elems_in_para) == 1:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n            else:\n                text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                for te in text_elems_in_para[1:]:\n                    te.text = \"\"\n            modified = True\n\n        # Replace follow-ups\n        for i, old_fu in enumerate(old_followups):\n            if old_fu in full_text:\n                new_fu = f\"{i+1}. {followups[i]['item']}\"\n                new_text = full_text.replace(old_fu, new_fu)\n                if len(text_elems_in_para) == 1:\n                    text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                else:\n                    text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                    for te in text_elems_in_para[1:]:\n                        te.text = \"\"\n                modified = True\n                break\n\n        if modified:\n            modified_p_ids.add(para_id)\n            # Remove layout cache elements from this paragraph\n            remove_layout_cache(para, ns_map)\n\n    # Also handle table cells (hp:tc elements contain hp:subList with hp:p)\n    for tc in root.iter(f'{{{hp_ns}}}tc'):\n        for sublist in tc.iter(f'{{{hp_ns}}}subList'):\n            for para in sublist.iter(f'{{{hp_ns}}}p'):\n                para_id = para.get('id')\n                text_elems_in_para = []\n\n                for run in para.iter(f'{{{hp_ns}}}run'):\n                    for t_elem in run.iter(f'{{{hp_ns}}}t'):\n                        if t_elem.text:\n                            text_elems_in_para.append(t_elem)\n\n                if not text_elems_in_para:\n                    continue\n\n                full_text = \"\".join(te.text or \"\" for te in text_elems_in_para)\n                modified = False\n\n                # Replace pricing band in table\n                if old_band in full_text:\n                    new_text = full_text.replace(old_band, update_data['band'])\n                    if len(text_elems_in_para) == 1:\n                        text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                    else:\n                        text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                        for te in text_elems_in_para[1:]:\n                            te.text = \"\"\n                    modified = True\n\n                # Replace escalation in table\n                elif old_escalation in full_text:\n                    new_text = full_text.replace(old_escalation, update_data['escalation_email'])\n                    if len(text_elems_in_para) == 1:\n                        text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                    else:\n                        text_elems_in_para[0].text = new_text\n                        for te in text_elems_in_para[1:]:\n                            te.text = \"\"\n                    modified = True\n\n                if modified:\n                    modified_p_ids.add(para_id)\n                    remove_layout_cache(para, ns_map)\n\n    # Write the modified XML back with proper XML declaration\n    tree.write(xml_file, encoding='UTF-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n    return modified_p_ids\n\n\ndef create_updated_hwpx(extracted_dir, output_path):\n    \"\"\"Create the updated HWPX file from the extracted directory.\"\"\"\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(output_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zipf:\n        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(extracted_dir):\n            for file in files:\n                file_path = os.path.join(root, file)\n                arcname = os.path.relpath(file_path, extracted_dir)\n                zipf.write(file_path, arcname)\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Read input data\n    update_data, followups = read_inputs()\n    print(f\"Update data: {update_data}\")\n    print(f\"Followups: {followups}\")\n\n    # Setup directories\n    extracted_dir = '/tmp/hwpx_work/extracted'\n    os.makedirs(extracted_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    #
